He asked the driver about the fare, Noor about the examination, and Mara whether a duke charged more for a rescue than an ordinary man.","\"Usually,\" Mara said. \"But I have negotiated a substantial discount.\"","Cassian, who was sitting on the edge of the examination bed under Noor's stern supervision, looked at her. \"Have you?\"","\"We are paying you in potatoes.\"","Ewan laughed. Noor used the little interruption to ask permission for the next part of her examination. Whatever the equipment on the tanker had done, she would not imitate the manufacturer's habit of treating a frightened person as a convenient collection of measurements.","In the meantime, June had found the port company's press statement. It described the incident as a demonstration of its commitment to safety. The release thanked senior management for arranging the prompt withdrawal of experimental machinery. It did not mention the crew who had disabled the generator, the operator who had called for an independent review, or Ewan.","\"A remarkable economy of gratitude,\" June said.","\"Do not put his name in the article,\" Mara told her.","\"I wasn't going to.\"","\"I know. I wanted him to hear us agree.\"","Ewan looked from one woman to the other. His shoulders lowered, almost imperceptibly.","The initial examination was inconclusive. Ewan could recall some parts of the trial vividly and others only in fragments. Fatigue, medication, fear, and a device he did not understand might all have affected his account. Noor documented what he reported without promising a diagnosis. She also arranged a quiet room, clean clothes, and a meal. Occasionally, the most useful response to uncertainty was a specific act of care.","At seven o'clock Rowan appeared with a briefcase and the air of a man who had spent an unpleasant afternoon being polite. He asked Cassian whether he had recovered enough to be objectionable.","\"I am improving,\" Cassian said.","\"A relief to everyone except your friends.\"","Rowan distributed copies of three financial statements. The same medical unit appeared in each at a different market value. One statement treated it as an investment in industrial development. Another called it a charitable lease. A third assigned the hospital an outstanding debt for equipment that the hospital had never been permitted to inspect.","\"Is that fraud?\" Mara asked.","\"It is an inconsistency,\" Rowan said. \"Fraud requires facts we do not yet have. I can tell you which questions the statements avoid.\"","He drew three columns on the back of an advertisement. In the first he wrote who paid; in the second, who held the title; in the third, who could control the equipment. Mara had expected the three answers to connect. They did, but through a chain of companies that crossed the bay twice before returning to an office above an empty shop.","\"Does the shop exist?\"","\"The house exists. The operator of the company does not trade there. Its registered occupation is the distribution of educational movies.\"","\"An interesting choice for a manufacturer.\"","\"A subsidiary may serve several functions. That is not itself improper. But if we oversimplify the structure, we will accuse the wrong company and give the right one time to move the records.\"","Mara studied the chart. The phrase economic activity had seemed solid when printed above the port gate. On Rowan's paper it broke into individual acts: a sailor earning a pay packet, a technician signing a form, a financial officer deciding which company would take in the liability. Economics became harder to dismiss as distant theory when the abstractions had names and rent to pay.","There were exemptions for research materials, a proposed service tax on auxiliary testing, and a clawback provision attached to the charitable contribution. If Mercy House challenged the supplier publicly, the supplier could demand the return of several years of support.","\"They made generosity reversible,\" Mara said.","\"They made the reversal depend on a condition they could influence,\" Rowan replied. \"That is more specific.\"","Outside the window, a delivery boy struggled to carry a bundle up the stairs. Cassian stood before Mara could stop him. She caught his sleeve.","\"Sit down. I will help.\"","\"It weighs almost nothing.\"","\"Then it will not miss you.\"","Rowan covered a smile with his hand. Cassian sat.","The bundle contained copies of the port's old maintenance chronicle. Leah's brother had asked a former colleague to collect them from a warehouse. The colleague had decided to deliver them individually rather than send them through the company's internal mail. His note said that a machine could decay for a long time before its failure became profitable to notice.","Mara found the entry for the medical unit's first installation. A predecessor model had been tested beside a grain mill. The stated purpose was to monitor the sustained attention of night operators. A later presentation claimed the device could assess a much broader field of behavior. The gap between those claims was not bridged by the documents they had.","\"Here,\" she said.","One page listed the unit's signal-processing chip. The batch number matched a component in an instrument used to measure water quality. An Italian laboratory had rejected the batch after a routine test. The reason was not radioactivity or a nuclear hazard. It was a subtle error that appeared only after prolonged exposure to heat.","\"Do we have the laboratory's actual report?\" Cassian asked.","\"A summary.\"","\"Then we ask for the report.\"","Mara nodded. She had begun to learn that an alarming conclusion was no less seductive than a reassuring one. Both could make her reluctant to ask an inconvenient question.","The phone on Rowan's desk rang. He answered, listened, and wrote down a number. His expression changed.","\"The supplier has filed notice of the clawback,\" he said.","\"Already?\"","\"Apparently the incident on the ship constitutes reputational harm.\"","For a moment the room seemed to shrink around the figures. Mara thought of the hospital's new purifier, the repaired lift, the children's room that had finally stopped smelling of damp. A contract could turn all those improvements into a means of control without changing a single thing they physically did.","She looked at Cassian. \"How much?\"","He told her.","Her first response was a quiet, exhausted kind of anger. Her second was to calculate what she could sell. The silver flower would not cover even a fringe of the debt. His house might. The thought was so absurdly tempting that she nearly laughed.","\"No,\" he said.","\"I haven't asked anything.\"","\"I know the expression.\"","\"You cannot forbid me to think.\"","\"I was refusing the idea that you must solve this before anyone else is allowed to be frightened.\"","She looked away. It was an unfairly gentle thing to say. She could have defended herself against an order.","Rowan closed the briefcase. \"We need a reserve fund while the claim is disputed. I can approach the smaller creditors. June can establish what was actually said publicly. Leah can preserve the original log. Nobody has to imagine a grand rescue before we try the ordinary measures.\"","\"And me?\" Mara asked.","\"You could eat something,\" said three people at once.","She glared at them. Then she took the bread Cassian offered.","Below the window, a group of port workers arrived carrying folding chairs. They had heard about the claim. One man had brought a tin of coins; another, a crate of wheat flour; a third, a broken electrical heater he hoped Ivo might equip for the children's room.","It was not enough. It was also more than Mara had expected to receive without being asked to become somebody else.","Among the papers in Rowan's case lay an unsigned authorization for him to freeze a family account. He had not mentioned it. Mara did not mention it either. She merely caught his eye and nodded.","He smiled as if she had passed him a secret across an exceptionally formal dinner.","At the door, Cassian waited while she finished her bread. His hand was open. She put hers in it.","The debt remained. For the first time that evening, it did not feel as though it belonged to her alone."],"questions":[{"type":"态度","stem":"Mara regards an alarming conclusion as seductive because it Career","volume":"卷四｜盐与钢的王冠","anchor":"chapter-0042","paragraphs":["On Saturday the port workers organized a volleyball match to raise money for Mercy House. It was an eccentric response to a financial institution's demand for immediate repayment. It also brought three hundred people to the beach, persuaded a retailer to donate food, and made it difficult for the city's newspapers to describe the strike as the hobby of a few idle troublemakers.","June had designed the poster. In large letters it promised entertainment. In smaller letters it promised that no patient would be photographed. The second promise cost her a sponsor before the first ball had been served.","\"He wanted a brave little girl in a hospital bed,\" she told Mara.","\"Could he accept a rude adult standing up?\"","\"You were my counteroffer.\"","The sponsor had declined. Another shop owner had quietly supplied enough sandwiches to feed everyone on the court. A public donation, June observed, was not the only kind that mattered.","Cassian arrived carrying the hospital's folding chairs. Mara had asked him to dress like an ordinary spectator. He had chosen a white shirt that made several entirely ordinary spectators walk into one another.","\"You did that impulsively,\" she said.","\"I dressed?\"","\"You wore sleeves you could roll up.\"","He looked at her, then slowly rolled the second sleeve. Rowan, standing behind him, made a small, delighted sound.","\"A divisive tactic,\" Rowan said. \"Some of us have been relying on personality.\"","Rook was keeping score. She wore the rubber goose from her cabin photograph on a cord around her neck. Anyone who objected to her decisions was invited to submit a written complaint to the goose. So far none had been upheld.","The teams combined port laborers, nurses, shop assistants, and people whose only qualification was a desire to be on the winning side. Ivo refused to play. He had spent the morning repairing the community hall's heating and considered that a sufficient contribution to sport. Anika sat beside him with two cups of tea. Mara was glad to see that he could talk to her without mentioning the water beneath the tanker.","At first Cassian watched. Then one of the nurses hurt her ankle, and the captain of her team pointed at him.","\"You look reasonably healthy.\"","\"A recent medical judgment differs,\" he said.","Noor, standing beside the court, folded her arms. \"You may stand at the back. You may not pretend that falling on your head is a form of exercise.\"","His athletic career began with a serve that went directly into the sea.","The crowd's roar was so affectionate that Mara almost forgave the ball for missing her.","\"An unfamiliar target,\" he said when she retrieved it.","\"The court is a broad rectangle, Cassian. We can put a diamond around it if that helps.\"","\"I recognize a double standard.\"","\"You recognized the coast with remarkable accuracy.\"","He was better on his second attempt. By the end of the game, a boy beside the canteen had revised his opinion of dukes from useless to not entirely useless. Cassian accepted the distinction with a grave inclination of his head.","Mara laughed until her sides hurt. For a little while, the investigation did not occupy the whole space of her attention. She ate a sandwich. She helped a nurse find a child's missing tooth in the sand. She listened to Rook describe a freight clerk who had tried to conceal heroin in a shipment labeled EDUCATIONAL SPONGES and had been caught because the buyer had ordered rubber, not sponge. Rook did not confuse the absurdity of the paperwork with the seriousness of the crime. She had known the clerk's family.","By the time the teams changed sides, the donation box was heavy with coins and almost empty of large notes. Mara counted it with the hall's treasurer, witnessed the total, and signed the sheet. The amount would keep the clinic's heating on for a week. It would not settle the supplier's claim.","The distinction could have made the afternoon feel small. Instead it gave the money a shape she could understand: one more week in which cold patients would not have to wear their coats indoors.","A man in a stylish grey suit approached the table. He introduced himself as a marketer for the port company. His employer wished to match the afternoon's donations, provided the hospital agreed to a joint photograph and a short statement acknowledging management's generous response.","\"Which response?\" June asked.","He hesitated. \"To the recent unfortunate events.\"","\"The unpaid wages, the unsafe equipment, or the repayment demand?\"","\"We hoped to focus on something positive.\"","Mara looked at the cheque he offered. She could feel the exact weight of the heating bill in her mind.","\"We will accept an unrestricted contribution,\" she said. \"The receipt will accurately identify the donor. The hospital will not endorse a statement about the dispute.\"","\"Then it would not achieve our purpose.\"","\"It would achieve ours.\"","He called somebody from the far corner of the beach. June watched him without raising her camera. A public relations employee could be part of a problem without deserving to become the afternoon's entertainment.","When he returned, the cheque was gone. He gave the treasurer a smaller sum in cash.","\"Personally,\" he said.","Nobody applauded. Mara thought he looked relieved.","At the table, June showed her the draft article. It distinguished the company's paid media campaign from independent reporting. A note in a marketing presentation called the space on the company's customer website sold media: advertisers could buy access to an audience that had come there for other reasons. The terminology was less interesting than the arrangement. Several apparently unrelated stories praising the medical trial had been published in space purchased by the same intermediary.","That did not mean every favorable account was false. It meant readers needed information that the presentation had omitted.","\"Will the paper publish the ownership links?\" Mara asked.","\"If I can verify them. We do not have to disprove every sentence before we explain who paid to place it.\"","A gust of wind lifted the pages. Cassian caught the top sheet. When he returned it to June, Mara noticed the white line where his shirt cuff had protected his wrist from the sun. It was ridiculous to find that intimate. She found it intimate anyway.","\"You enjoyed yourself,\" she said as he helped her carry a chair.","\"I have been reliably informed that I improved.\"","\"You nearly scored.\"","\"For which team?\"","She grinned. He looked at her for a moment longer than the joke required.","Then Ivo called from the canteen. Anika had received a message from the tanker. A maintenance engineer had agreed to meet them on the island where the trial equipment had previously been stored. He could produce the original revision history. He would not send it over the company network.","\"A credible source?\" Cassian asked.","\"An engineer I have worked with for eleven years,\" Anika said. \"That makes him known to me. It does not make every record he possesses genuine.\"","They agreed to examine the material without promising to publish it. Rook checked the marine forecast and said they could cross in the morning, provided they returned before the afternoon weather changed.","Mara felt the afternoon's warmth recede. An island, a private record, and a narrow window in which to leave: the arrangement had the familiar shape of a bad idea.","Cassian carried the last chair towards the hall. She walked beside him.","\"We should write down the plan,\" she said.","\"And tell Noor.\"","\"She will disapprove.\"","\"Probably.\"","Mara looked at him. \"You are getting inconveniently reasonable.\"","\"I have had companionship of an improving kind.\"","His smile was brief, private, and unfair. She spent the rest of the walk trying to think of a reply that did not reveal how thoroughly it had worked."],"questions":[{"type":"词句理解","stem":"The money giving the afternoon a “shape” chiefly means It did, however, appear in a real estate brochure, where the old power plant had become a marine research retreat and its loading dock an exclusive seaside promenade. Mara compared the photograph with the shore in front of her. The photographer had worked from a position that concealed the rusting tube above the water and the cable hanging from the cliff.","\"A valuable skill,\" she said. \"Making a place disappear while selling it.\"","Rook brought the boat against the dock. A steel ladder led to a gate whose little sign said PRIVATE. Beyond it, the wind moved through an orchard planted before the site had become industrial. Green fruit lay beneath the trees like fallen coins.","Anika had remained aboard the tanker to oversee repairs. Her colleague on the island was a woman called Tessa Roe, who had promised to meet them beside a yellow crane. There was no yellow crane. There was a fresh patch of paint on a slab of concrete.","Ivo stared at it. \"That was a very efficient removal.\"","\"How efficient?\" Cassian asked.","\"It takes more than a morning to dismantle one safely. Unless somebody planned this before she called.\"","Rook checked the sky. \"Forty minutes. I mean it.\"","Mara climbed the ladder. Cassian followed in a faded cap and the dark jacket of a cargo inspector. She had told him that an ordinary employee would not bring three changes of clothes to an island. He had replied that an ordinary employee might appreciate dry socks. The spare pair was now in her bag, along with a screwdriver she had accused him of packing because it matched his eyes.","He had not denied it.","Inside the gate, they found a directory whose most recent page had been taped over an older one. Beneath the retreat's logo, Mara could make out the words SIGNAL LABORATORY. A separate listing identified a geological collection and a room once used by a visiting mineralogist. The brochure had not mentioned either. A place could have a history more complicated than its current owner's taste in adjectives.","A man emerged from the reception building carrying a notebook. He looked at their jackets, then at Cassian's empty hands.","\"Inspection? Nobody notified me.\"","\"That is often the drawback,\" Mara said before Cassian could answer. \"People become remarkably tidy when they are notified.\"","The man was too tired to be suspicious of her cheerfulness. He asked for a number. Mara gave him the genuine maintenance reference Anika had supplied. He checked it against his list and waved them through.","She had not invented a qualification. She had allowed him to decide what the number meant. The difference might comfort a philosopher. It would not comfort Cassian if she got them arrested.","\"You could have told me,\" he murmured as they crossed the courtyard.","\"You were about to tell him your name.\"","\"It has occasionally been useful.\"","\"So has mine. Usually to make people lock things.\"","The building smelled of hot metal and the sea. Its long corridor ended in a studio fitted with cameras, lights, and a raised platform. A broad window overlooked the ocean. Mara recognized the background from the manufacturer's presentation: the same calm horizon, the same reassuring blue. The machinery shown in the film had been assembled here, not at a hospital.","Ivo examined a projector. Its portable battery was missing. On the floor lay a torn corner of a printed profile. Mara could read a sentence about a foreign-born technician whose work had helped the company improve its workplace monitor. Beneath the flattering description, someone had added a private note: DO NOT INCLUDE THE SECOND TEST.","\"Tessa?\" Cassian called.","No answer came. Then something tapped twice inside a metal storage cabinet.","Mara reached it before either man could speak. The lock had a narrow, old-fashioned keyhole. New paint covered a repair above the hinge. She pressed the panel, felt it give, and pushed one corner of the frame upward. The repair had made the cabinet look secure without restoring its structure.","\"Cheap work,\" she said.","\"Remind me not to employ the contractor,\" Cassian replied.","The panel came loose. Tessa Roe fell forward into his arms, coughing and furious. Someone had tied her wrists with a plastic strap. Her first words were not a request for water. They were a precise, remarkably imaginative description of Director Pell's ancestry.","Mara liked her at once.","Tessa had reached the island before dawn. The records she had promised were still on the original workstation. She had been preparing a copy when two men arrived with an order to clear the building. Her objection had earned her the cabinet. The men said the site would be demolished because of erosion.","\"They cannot get permission that quickly,\" Ivo said.","\"The cliff is old. The application is not. They have been waiting for a convenient morning.\"","Tessa rubbed her wrists. She wanted to lead them to the workstation. Cassian wanted to call Rook. Mara wanted to leave before the men returned. For several seconds they had the kind of argument in which everyone was right and time was being wasted.","\"Ten minutes,\" Mara said. \"We go together, we take what is already there, and we do not hunt for anything else.\"","\"Eight,\" said Cassian.","She took his hand and pulled him towards the door. \"I enjoy your optimism.\"","The workstation stood beside an automatic sample carrier, its cable tied in a neat loop. Tessa entered her own credentials. A directory opened. Mara watched a list of files appear, each with a date, a trial number, and a short description. One column tracked equipment changes. Another recorded complaints from the people wearing the device.","\"The word fatigue disappears after this revision,\" Tessa said. \"Look at the new heading.\"","RECALL CONSISTENCY.","Ivo photographed the screen. Cassian asked Tessa to include the underlying files, not only the summary. She began to copy them to a storage drive. The progress bar moved slowly enough to feel malicious.","Outside, an engine started.","Mara went to the window. A truck was backing towards the loading bay. The men beside it were not carrying office boxes. One had a fuel can; another, a metal cutting tool. The demolition notice lay under a clipboard on the dashboard.","She looked at the progress bar. Eleven percent.","\"We need a distraction,\" she said.","Cassian followed her gaze. \"A less explosive one than you are considering.\"","\"You don't know what I am considering.\"","\"I have observed a pattern.\"","She kissed his cheek. It was quick, entirely deliberate, and effective enough to interrupt the next sentence.","\"Then observe this one. I am going downstairs. You are keeping Tessa with you. If I am not back in two minutes, bring everyone to the dock.\"","His fingers closed around hers for a moment. \"Two minutes. I will be counting.\"","Mara took the nearest clipboard. On the way out she picked up the project's visitor badges. One read SENIOR REVIEWER. The name underneath was a man's.","She turned the badge over.","At the loading bay, the driver looked up in annoyance. \"We were told the site was empty.\"","\"You were also told where to put the equipment?\" Mara asked.","He glanced at the truck. \"Of course.\"","\"Then you can explain why it is facing the wrong direction.\"","He climbed out of the cab. She stepped back, gave him room, and began to ask a series of exquisitely irritating questions about the clearance beneath the door. He answered the first three. By the fourth, he was measuring it. By the fifth, he was arguing with the man who had brought the cutting tool.","Mara could see the studio window above them. Cassian appeared behind the glass. He held up one finger.","She gave the driver a sympathetic smile.","\"I am sure the specifications are perfectly clear,\" she said. \"Once we find the right version.\"","The man with the fuel can stopped listening to the argument. He was looking at her face. Mara knew the instant recognition arrived.","Above her, Cassian vanished from the window.","So much for the second minute."],"questions":[{"type":"细节","stem":"What turns the island visit into an
